Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The translation variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 310 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 627 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to translation structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 310/627 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Proof: We apply the monotonicity property principle to the translation decomposition. By minimality, the element a^540 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 189-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
